- **rete-from-graph**: Build a queryable `.rete` file from ANY graph-based data source — an RDF dump (.nt/.nq/.ttl/.rdf/.owl), a SPARQL endpoint, a Wikibase, an OWL ontology, GeoJSON, tabular/CSV, a GBIF Darwin-Core archive, a TEI corpus, or a JSON API. - **rete-local-query**: Query a LOCAL .rete file efficiently — the lazy range-read path that opens a multi-GB file without loading it whole, which CLI commands are lazy vs read-whole, the tuning knobs (RETE_BLOCK_KB, RETE_LOCAL_LAZY_ABOVE_MB, the 256 MiB block-cache cap), how to preview a query's byte/RAM cost (rete cost / rete why), how to prove a memory bound, and the real memory-scaling limits at billion-triple scale. Use whenever a local query is slow, OOMs, or you need to bound/preview its I/O and RAM. Complements rete-sparql (which is about writing the query).
